cūttiram: 12
tiṇaimayak kuṟutaluṅ kaṭinilai yilavē;
nilaṉoruṅku mayaṅkuta liṉṟeṉa moḻipa
pulaṉaṉ kuṇarnta pulamai yōrē.
karuttu: ituvum itaiyaṭutta uripporuḷallaṉa eṉṉuñ cūttiramum muṉ aintāvatu cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya mullai mutaliya nilavakaikaḷukkum, piṉ puṇartal pirital eṉum 14āvatu cūttirattiṟ kūṟum kuṟiñci mutaliya tiṇaivakai kaḷukkumuḷḷa iyaipu muraṇkaḷai, naṭunilaiviḷakkāy niṉṟu, eṭuttukkāṭṭi aiyamakaṟṟa eḻunta cūttiraṅkaḷākum.poruḷ: tiṇai mayakkuṟutaluṅ kaṭinilai yilavē = kuṟiñci mutaliya aṉpiṉain tiṇaikaḷāṉa puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭal eṉṟa aintoḻukkaṅkaḷum tattamakkuc ciṟappurimai yuṭaiya muṟaiyē kuṟiñci pālai mullai neytal marutam eṉṉum nilaṅkaḷil nikaḻvatuṭaṉ, avvāṟu ciṟappurimai yaṟṟa piṟa nilaṅkaḷil vantu kalattalum vilakkappaṭā; nilaṉoruṅku mayaṅkutal iṉṟeṉa moḻipa = (ivvāṟoḻukkaṅkaḷ tamak kurimaiyaṟṟa nilaṅkaḷil nikaḻntu mayaṅkutalamaiyu meṉiṉum) avvoḻukka mayakkam paṟṟit tammuḷ nilaṅkaḷ mayaṅkutalillai yeṉṟu kūṟuvar; pulaṉ naṉkuṇarnta pulamaiyōrē = pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkaṅkaḷai naṉkaṟinta aṟivuṭaiyōr.
kuṟippu: itaṉ mutal mūṉṟāmaṭikaḷiṉ īṟṟēkāraṅkaḷ acainilai. ummai tiṇaikkum mayakkam ciṟappiṉmai uṇarttum. itaṉāl ovvoru nilattiṟkum ovvorutiṇai ciṟappurimai yuṭaiyateṉpatum, avvurimai muṟaiyaṉṟi nilaṅkaḷun tiṇai kaḷum tammuḷ mayaṅkutal ciṟavāteṉiṉum vilakkumāṟillai eṉpatum teḷiyappaṭum. mayakkam, taṉakkuc ciṟappurimaiyaṟṟa nilattil piṟa oḻukka nikaḻcci kuṟikkum; ennilattum tiṇaikaḷ tammuṭṭā mayaṅkutal kūṭāmaiyiṉ, atu karuttaṉmai tēṟṟam. oruṅku, uṭaṉikaḻccip poruṭṭu.
akattuṟaikaḷil mutalil oḻukkaṅkaḷukkē uriya kuṟiñci mutaliya peyarkaḷ piṉ niralē avvoḻukkaṅkaḷukkuc ciṟanturiya nilaṅkaḷukkum ākupeyarāy vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa. avaikaḷ nilaṅ kaḷukkē uriya peyarkaḷeṉal piṟkālap piṟaḻvuṇarcci. eṉavē, ciṟappuk kuṟiyākap puṇartal mutaliya aintiṇaikaḷaiyum, āku peyarāy avaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟanturiya nilaṅkaḷaiyum, kuṟiñci pālai mullai neytal marutam eṉum potuppeyarkaḷāṟ kūṟutal paḻaiya tamiḻ nūṉ marapu. eṉil, peyaroṟṟumaiyāl tiṇai kaḷum nilaṅkaḷum yāṇṭum oru nīrmaiya veṉak koṇṭu, tiṇaimayaṅkuntōṟum nilamayakkamum uṇṭeṉak koḷḷutal kūṭāteṉa vaṟpuṟutti aiyamakaṟṟuvatē īṇṭiccūttirak karuttākum. tiṇaiyeṉpatu oḻukkattiṟkē ciṟanturiya peyarākum. tiṇaik kuripporuḷē eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarum itaṉai valiyuṟuttum. ovvōroḻukkamum avvavataṟkuc ciṟappurimai kūṟiya oru nilattiṟkē taṉiyurimai yuṭaittaṉṟu. ovvoru nilamakkaḷum avvan nilattil aintiṇaiyoḻukkaṅkaḷaiyum kaiyāḷutaliyalpu. ākavē, ovvoru nilattum atuvataṟkuc ciṟanturiya oḻukkamē yaṉṟip piṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷaiyum toṭarpupaṭuttip pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkañceytal tavaṟākātu. oru nilattu nikaḻum oḻukka vēṟu pāṭṭāl annilattiyalpum māṟiṉatākak karutalākātu. mayaṅki nikaḻum oḻukkattōṭu oruṅkē nilamayakkaṅ koḷḷutal marapaṉṟu eṉpatum, ovvoru nilattiṟkuc ciṟappurimaiyuṭaiyatākak kūṟappaṭiṉum atukoṇṭu  annilattu avvōr oḻukkamē nikaḻa vēṇṭum eṉṟa varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟi nilattiyal karutāmal tiṇaimayakkam (atāvatu piṟavoḻukka nikaḻcci) kūṟutalum marapeṉpatum, tolkāppiyar iccūttirattāl viḷakkip pōntār. eṉavē, eṅku evvoḻukkam nikaḻiṉum ataṉāl nilattiyal māṟātu; kāṭu puṇarcci nikaḻvatāl kuṟiñciyākātu, mullai nilamēyām. pirivāṟṟāk kuṟamakaḷiriraṅkuvatāl malai nilam neytalākātu; kuṟiñciyēyām.
tattam iyalmāṟāta nilam yātāyiṉum, atil nikaḻum oḻukkampaṟṟit tiṇai vakuppatu paḻaiya marapu; oḻukka iyal karutātu, nilavakaiyāl tiṇaiyamaippatu piḻaipaṭṭa piṟkāla vaḻakku. atu tolkāppiyarkkuṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu eṉpatu ic cūttirattāl teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. puṇartal - mutaliya ovvoru tiṇai allatu oḻukkattiṟkum, muṟaiyē malai mutaliya nilaṅ kaḷaip pōlavē, kūtiryāma mutaliya kāla vakaikaḷum tattam iyaṟporuttam paṟṟit taṉicciṟappurimai koḷḷumeṉiṉum, oru tiṇaikku orē kālantāṉ kūṟal vēṇṭumeṉum varaiyaṟai
yillai. entat tiṇaiyum taṉ iyalpaṟṟi ataṟkuc ciṟanturiya tallāta piṟanilam piṟa kālaṅkaḷil nikaḻtal kūṭumātaliṉ, ovvoru tiṇaiyum ellānilaṅkaḷilum pōlavē ellāk kālaṅkaḷoṭum kalantu nikaḻvatum kaṭiyappaṭātu eṉpatum veḷippaṭai. eṉavē, ovvōroḻukkamum ataṟkuc ciṟanturiya tākak kūṟappeṟṟa nilam poḻutukaḷilēyē nikaḻum eṉa oru talaiyāk koḷḷal kūṭātu; uripporuḷkaḷākiya ellā oḻukkaṅkaḷum, nilamum poḻutumākiya mutaṟporuḷ vakaikaḷil orōvoṉṟait tattamakku iyaliyaipupaṟṟic ciṟappurimai koḷḷiṉum, ellā nilaṅkaḷiṉum ellāp poḻutukaḷiṉum ettiṇaiyum ēṟṟa peṟṟi kalantu nikaḻtaluṅ kaṭiyap paṭātu eṉpatē iccūttirak karuttātal teḷivākum. iṉi, iccūttirattil mutaṟporuḷiraṇṭil poḻutukūṟāmal nilam vitantu kūṟap paṭṭatu. poḻutu pōlaṉṟit tiṇaiyoṭu nilam kuṟiñci mutaliya potuppeyar peṟṟu mayaṅkutalāl varum aiyamakaṟṟaṟ poruṭṭu, tiṇaiyoṭu peyaroṟṟumaiyillāta poḻutukaḷ paṟṟi attakaiya mayakkattiṟ kiṭaṉillaiyātaliṉ, poḻutu mayaṅkāmai vitantu kūṟavēṇṭiṟṟillai.
itaṟku oru nilattu iraṇṭuripporuḷ, atāvatu iraṇ ṭoḻukkam tammuṉ mayaṅkutalaṉṟi, iraṇṭu nilam orō voḻukkattiṉkaṇ mayaṅkutalillai eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. mēlum, uripporuḷ mayakkuṟutal eṉṉātu, tiṇai mayakkuṟutalumeṉṟār, ōruripporuḷoṭu ōrurip poruḷ mayaṅkutalum.... ivvāṟē kālamayaṅkutalum, karup poruḷ mayaṅkutalum peṟumeṉṟaṟku eṉac ciṟappukkuṟippum kūṭṭivirittār. itaṉāl oru nilattorukālattu oṉṟaṉmikka palatiṇaikaḷ mayaṅkumeṉpatu karuttāyiṉ, atu kūṭāmai tēṟṟam. aṉṟi orutiṇai pala nilattum mayaṅkum iyalpaiyē ivvāṟu kūṟiṉar eṉiṉ, atu mikaiyāvatōṭu vēṟupāṭillāta oru māṟupāṭṭait tāṉēpaṭaittu mayaṅkuvatumākum. ovvoru nilattum ataṟkuriya vallāp piṟavoḻukkaṅkaḷ nikaḻtalamaiyum  eṉpataṉālēyē, ovvōroḻukkattiṟkum ataṟkuriya nilamē yaṉṟip piṟanilaṅkaḷaiyun toṭarpupaṭuttik kūṟutal amaivuṭait teṉpatum teḷiyappaṭum. oru nilattup pala voḻukkam nikaḻum eṉṟapiṉ, palanilattu ōroḻukkam nikaḻum (atāvatu ovvō roḻukkattōṭum pala nilattoṭarpu  amaiyum) eṉpatai vilakkumāṟillai.eṉavē, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatē īṇṭuk karuttāyiṉ, tiṇaimayakkuṟutaluṅ kaṭinilaiyilavē eṉṟa aḷavē akkaruttai viḷakkap pōtiyatākum; nilaṉoruṅku mayaṅkutaliṉṟu eṉak kūṭṭiyuraippatu poruṭ poruttamiṉṟi muraṇpāṭum viḷaittu mayaṅka vaikkum. iṉi, oruppaṭṭa talaimakkaḷuḷ ōroḻukkam pala nilaṅkaḷil nikaḻvatu oru kālat tamaiyātu eṉṟu kūṟi, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraikku amaivu kāṭṭuvatum poruntātu. orēkālattum orunilattum pala voḻukkam oruṅku nikaḻtalum, oruppaṭṭa orē talaimakkaḷuḷ ōroḻukkam orukālattup palaviṭattu nikaḻtalum kūṭāmai veḷippaṭai. ataṉālum atu poruḷaṉmaiyaṟika.
itaṟku iḷampūraṇar kūṟum poruḷāvatu: oru tiṇaik kuriya mutaṟporuḷ maṟṟōr tiṇaikkuriya mutaṟporuḷoṭu cēraniṟṟaluṅ kaṭiyappaṭātu; āṇṭu nilam cēraniṟṟalillai yeṉṟu colluvar; eṉavē kālam mayaṅkum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu eṉpatē. cūttirattil oru nilattiṟkuriya voḻukkam vēṟoru nilattiṟkuk kūṟutalum kaṭinilaiyilaveṉat teḷḷatteḷiya viḷakki yirukkavum, ataṟku māṟāka orutiṇaikkuriya mutaṟ poruḷ maṟṟōr tiṇaikkuriya mutaṟporuḷōṭu cēra niṟṟal kūṭātu eṉa muraṇap poruḷ kūṟuvatu amaivuṭaittaṉṟu. tolkāppiyar tiṇaimayakkuṟutal eṉṟāraṉṟi, tiṇaiyil mutaṟporuḷ mayaṅkutal eṉṟu kūṟiṉārillai. ātaliṉ, iḷampūraṇar kūṟṟum iccūttirac coṟṟoṭarukku nēriya poruḷaṉṟu.
tiṇaikaḷaiyum nilaṅkaḷaiyum cuṭṭi avaṟṟiṉiyaipu viḷakka eḻunta iccūttirattil, avaṟṟiṉ puṟampāṉa kālaṅkaḷai yum karupporuḷkaḷaiyum pukutta vēṇṭip paḻaiya, uraikārar iccūttirattiṟkut tammuḷ māṟupaṭap palavāṟu mayakkattiṟ kiṭamāṉa poruntāp poruḷ kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
tiṇai mayakkuṟutaṟkuc ceyyuḷ.
	kaṭaṟkāṉaṟ cērppa! kaḻiyulāay nīṇṭa
	aṭaṟkāṉaṟ puṉṉaitāḻn tāṟṟa - maṭaṟkāṉal
	aṉṟi lakavum aṇineṭum peṇṇaittem
	muṉṟi liḷamaṇaṉmēl moyttu	(tiṇaimālai 190. cey. 56)
itil kuṟiñcit tiṇaiyiṉ iravukkuṟi nērumtuṟai, taṉakkuriya kuṟiñci nilattaṉṟi, iraṅkaṟṟiṇaikkuriya ney taṉilattu mayaṅkiyataṟika. avvāṟu kuṟiñcittiṇai vantu kalappiṉum, ataṉāl nilattiyal mayaṅkāmal neytalākavē niṟpatu kūṟap paṭutalum kāṇka. iṉṉum, pulālakaṟṟum eṉum tiṇaimālai (35ām) veṇpāvum neytaṉilattil kuṟiñcittiṇai mayakkam kūṟum. iṉi,
	puṉpuṟavē cēvalō ṭūṭal poruḷaṉṟāl,
	aṉpuṟa vēyuṭaiyā rāyiṉum - vaṉpuṟ
	ṟatukāṇ akaṉṟa vaḻinōkkip poṉpōrt
	titukāṇeṉ vaṇṇa miṉi	(tiṇaimālai 152l. cey. 74)
eṉum tiṇaimālaippāṭṭil neytaṟṟiṇai pālainilattu mayaṅkutal aṟika.
cūttiram: 13
uripporu ḷallaṉa mayaṅkavum peṟumē.
karuttu: itu aintiṇaikaḷ pōlavē, attiṇaikaḷiṉ cārpāṉa piṟa uripporuḷkaḷum pala nilaṅkaḷilum ēṟṟapeṟṟi vantu mayaṅkum, eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
poruḷ: uripporuḷallaṉa = tiṇaikkurip poruḷē eṉa varaiyaṟutta puṇartal mutal aintiṇaiyallāta uripporu ḷāṉa talaimakkaḷ oḻukkamāvaṉa piṟa; mayaṅkavumpeṟum = ennilattum vantu kalattalumuṇṭu.
kuṟippu: puṇartal, pirital, iruttal, iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉṉum tiṇaikkuripporuḷ aintumaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ cārpāy avvurip poruḷkaḷōṭu amaittuk kōṭaṟpālaṉavāy, piṟṟaic cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟum koṇṭutalaik kaḻital pirintava ṇiraṅkal, kalanta poḻutu. kāṭci mutaliyaṉavum, aintiṇai uripporuḷkaḷaip pōlavē oru nilattukkut taṉiyurimai yiṉṟi ellānilattum vantu mayaṅkavum peṟum. īṟṟēkāram acainilai.
tiṇaikkurip poruḷkaḷāka vitantu kūṟum puṇartal mutaliya aintum enta nilattum vantu mayaṅkutal mēṟ cūttirattiṟ kūṟap paṭṭatu. pēraḷavil avvaintaṉuḷaṭaṅkāmal, iyalpaṟṟi uripporuḷā yamaivaṉa piṟavumuḷa eṉpatai ivviyal koṇṭu talaikkaḻital kalantapoḻutum eṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷiṟ cuṭṭuvatāl, attakaiya piṟa akavurip poruḷkaḷum, vitantōtiya aintiṇai yuripporuḷkaḷ pōlavē, varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟi yenta nilattum vantu mayaṅkutal kūṭum eṉpatai intac cūttiram viḷakkukiṟatu. īṇṭu uripporuḷallaṉa eṉpatu muṉṉaic cūttirattil tiṇaikkuripporuḷ eṉa vitantōtiya talaimakkaḷoḻukkam aintu mallāta piṟaveṉak koḷḷal vēṇṭum. uripporu ḷallaṉa eṉpatai allaṉa uripporuḷ eṉac coṉmāṟṟi, muṉvitantōtiya aintumallāta uripporuḷāvaṉa eṉak koḷḷuvatē īṇṭup peritum poruttamuṭaittām.
iṉi, ivvāṟu koḷḷāmal, uripporuḷallāta karup poruḷum mutaṟporuḷum piṟatiṇaiyōṭu cērum eṉṟu iḷampūraṇar poruḷ koḷvatum poruntātu. ēṉeṉil, tiṇaimayakkuṟutalum eṉṟa cūttirattāl mutaṟ poruḷ mayakka mum ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvumpuḷḷum eṉpatāl karupporuḷ kaḷiṉ mayakkamum muṉ taṉi vēṟu cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, avaṟṟaiyē iccūttirattilum tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉāreṉpatu kūṟiyatu kūṟal eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟattiṟku avaraiyāḷākkum.
uripporuḷ eṉṟōtiya aintiṇaiyu mallāta kaik kiḷai peruntiṇaiyum nālvakai nilattum mayaṅkavum peṟum eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraiyum amaivuṭaittaṉṟu. kaik kiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷukku nilam poḻutukaḷil etuvumē taṉiyurimai kūṟātatāl, vilakkillā nilampoḻutukaḷil avai vantu mayaṅkumeṉac cuṭṭutal mikaiyākum. aṉṟiyum mēl iraṇṭāñ cūttira mutal kīḻ 42ām cūttiramuṭiya niralē naṭuvaṇain tiṇaikaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷaiyē viḷakkip, piṟaku 43 mutal 49 varaiyuḷḷa cūttiraṅkaḷil cila potuviyalpukaḷ kūṟi, avaṟṟiṉ piṉ ivviyaliṟutiyil 50, 51ām cūttiraṅkaḷil kaikkiḷai peruntiṇaikaḷai viḷakki muṭikkum innūlār īṇṭu aṉpiṉain tiṇaiyiṉ iyalpukaḷukkiṭaiyē iṉṟiyamaiyāt toṭarpu etuvumiṉṟi iṟutiyiṟ kūṟum kaikkiḷai peruntiṇai kaḷaiyiḻuttu avaṟṟiṉ ilakkaṇam kūṟumuṉ mayakkaṅ kūṟiṉā reṉak koḷḷutal evvāṟṟāṉuñ cālāmaiyaṟika.
aintiṇaiyallāp piṟa uripporuḷ palanilattu mayaṅkutaṟkuc ceyyuḷ:
koṇṭutalaikkaḻital attakaiya piṟa uripporuḷāta luṇṭeṉa aṭutta piṉ cūttiram kūṟukiṟatu. avvuripporuḷ puṇartaṟkuriya kuṟiñci nilattil mayaṅkuvatākak kīḻvarum paḻam pāṭṭuk kūṟutal kāṇka.
niṉaiyāy, vāḻi, tōḻi! eṉum pūtantēvaṉār kuṟumpāṭṭil,
		.............. pulippakuvā yēṟṟai
		.............viṭarmukaik
		kōṭai oṟṟiyakaruṅkālvēṅka
		vāṭupūñciṉaiyiṟkiṭakkum
		uyarvaranāṭaṉoṭupeyarumāṟē”(kuṟun. 343)
puli vēṅkaic ciṉaiyiṟ kiṭakku muyarvarai nāṭākavē nilam kuṟiñci; nāṭaṉoṭu peyartal koṇṭutalaik kaḻitalā muripporuḷ. iṉṉum ūuralareḻa eṉum peruṅkaṭuṅ kōviṉ pāṭṭu matuvātalaṟika.
	uṇalāyn ticiṉā lavaroṭu cēynāṭṭu
	viṇṭoṭa nivanta vilaṅkumalaik kavāṟ
	karumpunaṭu pātti yaṉṉa
	peruṅkaḻiṟ ṟaṭivaḻi nilaiiya nīrē           	(kuṟun.262)
itil, puṇartaṟkuriya malaiyiṟ ṟalaivaṉuṭaṉ pōkum talaivi kaḷiṟṟaṭi vaḻi nilaiiya nīrait talaivaṉō ṭuṇṇutalai virumpum oḻukkam mayaṅkutalaṟika.
iṉi, koṇṭutalaikkaḻital,
	aṉṉāy vāḻi vēṇṭaṉṉai, nampaṭappait
	tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya, avarnāṭ
	ṭuvalaik kūvaṟ kīḻa
	māṉuṇ ṭeñciya kaluḻi nīrē	(aiṅ. 203)
eṉum aiṅkuṟunūṟṟup pāṭṭil kuṟiñci nilattum,
	oṉṟāṉu nāmmoḻiya lāmō celavutāṉ
	piṉṟātu pēṇum pukaḻāṉpiṉ - piṉṟā
	velaṟkaritām vilvalāṉ vēlviṭalai pāṅkāc
	celaṟkaritāc cēya curam	(tiṇaimālai 150l cey. 87)
eṉum tiṇaimālai veṇpāvil pālainilattum, vantu mayaṅkutal kāṇka.
itupōlavē maṟṟaiya aintiṇaiyilaṭaṅkā uripporuḷ aṉaittum  ellānilattum mayaṅkumeṉṟaṟika.
cūttiram: 14
puṇartal pirita lirutta liraṅkal
ūṭa livaṟṟi ṉimitta meṉṟivai
tēruṅ kālait tiṇaikkurip poruḷē.
karuttu: iccūttiram akapporuṭ pakutiyiṟ ciṟanta aṉpiṉaintiṇaikku nērurimaikoṇṭa oḻukka vakaikaḷai uṇarttukiṟatu.
poruḷ: puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭalivaṟṟiṉ nimitta meṉṟivai = kūṭutal, pirital, piriviṭaiyāṟṟi yiruttal, āṟṟātiraṅkal, pulavi eṉṟa aintum avaṟṟiṟ kiyaipuṭaiya nimittaṅkaḷumē; tēruṅkālait tiṇaikkuripporuḷē = ārāyum poḻutu aṉpiṉaintiṇaiyeṉaṟkuc ciṟanturiya poruḷkaḷām.
kuṟippu: itil īṟṟēkāram tēṟṟamākum. mutal karu urip poruḷkaḷaṉaittum akattiṇaip pakutiyākavē amaittuk kōṭal tavaṟaṉṟāyiṉum, tiṇaiyeṉpatu oḻukkaṅkaṇṇiya pērātalāl aṉpiṉaintiṇai yeṉaṟkup puṇartal pirital mutaliya talaimakkaḷ aintoḻukkaṅkaḷē ciṟappurimaiyuṭaiya poruḷkaḷākum eṉpatai iccūttiram viḷakkukiṟatu.
nimittamāvatu ovvōroḻukkattai yaṭuttu muṉṉum piṉṉum mutalum muṭivumākat toṭuttu avvoḻukkattiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāt toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavaṟṟaic cuṭṭuvatām. pukumukam purital, nakunayam maṟaittal mutaliyaṉa puṇartalukku muṉṉeḻum nimittaṅkaḷām. aṇintavai tiruttal, pārāṭṭeṭuttal mutaliyaṉa puṇarviṉ piṉṉar nikaḻum nimittaṅkaḷām. ivvāṟu toṭar paṇimai yaṟṟaṉa nimittamākā.
aṉṉapiṟavum... nimittameṉpa eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiyal 19ām cūttiramum itai valiyuṟuttum. ivvāṟē piṟatiṇaikaḷukkum nimitta  vakaikaḷai ēṟṟa peṟṟi amaittuk koḷka. niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē muṉṉar mutaṟporuḷaik kūṟiṉavar ataṉai yaṭuttuk karupporuḷaik kūṟāmal ataṟkumuṉ īṇṭurip poruḷkaḷaik kūṟuvatēṉeṉiṟ colluvaṉ: akap pakutiyil mutaṟporuḷ uripporuḷkaḷ allātaṉa aṉaittum karup poruḷkaḷā yamaitaliṉ, mutalil varaiyaṟaippaṭṭavaṟṟai viḷakkuvāṉ ṟoṭaṅki, nilampoḻuteṉum iraṇṭē vakaiyu ḷaṭaṅkum mutaṟporuḷ kūṟiṉatum, aḷavaṟutippaṭāk karup poruḷkaḷaik kūṟumuṉ, aintu oḻukkattaḷavil aṭaṅkum urip poruḷai iṭaiyiṟkūṟiyamaittapeṟṟi uyttuṇara vaittār. akavakaic ceyyuḷkaḷaṉaittum ivvaintiṇaiyē kūṟutaliṉ, eṭuttukkāṭṭu īṇṭaikkuvēṇṭā.
cūttiram: 15
koṇṭutalaik kaḻitalum pirintava ṇiraṅkalum
uṇṭeṉa moḻipa ōriṭat tāṉa.
karuttu: itu, mēlaiccūttirattil tiṇaikkurip poru ḷeṉa viḷakkiya aintaṉuḷaṭaṅkātaṉavāyt, tammiyalpāl akat tiṇaikaḷuk kuripporuḷkaḷāy āṭci peṟum piṟa cilavum uṇṭeṉak kūṟukiṟatu.
poruḷ: koṇṭutalaik kaḻitalum = kaḷavil talaivaṉ talaiviyait taṉṉuṭaṉ koṇṭucēṟalum; pirintavaṇiraṅkalum  = avvāṟuṭaṉ koṇṭu celluṅkāl talaiviyiṉ tamarvaravu mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāl piriyanēriṉ āṇṭap pirivupaṟṟi yiraṅkalum; uṇṭeṉa moḻipa = ēṟṟapeṟṟi ivai urip poruḷpōla varutalum uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvar (pulavar); ōriṭat tāṉa = avai akattiṇaik kiyaipuṭaiya orōviṭattu.
kuṟippu: koṇṭutalaik kaḻital eṉpataṉāl, talaimakkaḷ tammuḷ atu piritalākātu; nērē puṇartalum aṉṟām. atu nilam peyartaliṉ puṇartaliṉ aṭaṅkāmaiyāṉum, uṭaṉ koṇṭu peyartaliṉ piritaliṉaṭaṅkāmaiyāṉum, vēṟu ōtap paṭṭatu eṉum iḷampūraṇar kuṟippu īṇṭuc cintikkattakkatu. iṉi, atuvē pōl pirintavaṇiraṅkal eṉpatum pirintavar iraṅkal eṉṉuntiṇaikkurip poruḷiṉ vēṟupaṭṭatu eṉpatai viḷakkutaṟkākavē, koṇṭutalaik kaḻiyumiṭattup piriya nērntuḻi avaṇ iraṅkal eṉa vitantu viḷakkappeṟṟatu. iccūttiram cuṭṭum iraṇṭum akattuṟaikaḷil uripporuḷkaḷākiya talai makkaḷiṉoḻukkamākavē āḷappeṟutalāṉum, ivai puṇartal pirital iruttal iraṅkal ūṭal eṉṟa tiṇaikkuripporuḷ kaḷaintaṉuḷ etaṉilum aṭaṅkāmaiyāṉum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum vēṟu pirittu, iccūttirattāl orōviṭattu uripporuḷātaṟku uriyaṉa ivaiyeṉṟu teḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ituvē tolkāppiyar karutteṉpatu piṉ ivviyalil oṉṟāttamariṉum eṉum (41ām) cūttirattil, iṭaic curamaruṅki lavaḷtamareytik kaṭaikkoṇṭu peyartaliṟ kalaṅkaña reyti eṉṟavar viḷakkutalāṉumaṟika.
īṇṭu iḷampūraṇar koṇṭutalaik kaḻitalaip pālaik kaṇṇum pirintavaṇiraṅkalaip peruntiṇaik kaṇṇum varum uripporuḷkaḷākkip poruḷ kūṟuvar. uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōtal (atāvatu koṇṭu talaikkaḻital) piriviṉaṭaṅkātu eṉa ivarē kūṟiyapiṉ, atu pirivām pālaik kaṇvarumeṉal, taṉṉoṭutāṉē māṟukoḷak kūṟuvatām. talaimakkaḷ tammuṭ pirintiraṅkuvatu nēriya neytaṟṟiṇaik kuripporuḷākum. pirintiraṅkal eṉaittāṉum peruntiṇaippāṟ paṭutalillai, atu ottakātaloḻukkamātaliṉ.
iṉi, ivviraṇṭaiyum tannilaiyil uripporuḷāk kāmal, tiṇaimayakkam eṉṟu koṇṭu pālaittiṇaiyuḷ kuṟiñciyum neytalumākiya piṟatiṇaikaḷiṉ mayakkam kūṟuvatākak koḷḷum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraiyum poruntātu. tiṇai mayakkuṟutal eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭarāl mayaṅki, orutiṇaiyuḷ piṟatiṇaikaḷ oruṅku vantu mayaṅkumeṉak koḷḷutal evvāṟ ṟāṉum poruntātu. talaimakkaḷiruvaruḷ orukālattōroḻukkam nikaḻvataṉṟip palavoḻukkam orukālattukkalantu mayaṅku meṉpatu iyalpaṉṟākaliṉ, ataṟkilakkaṇamum vēṇṭappaṭā. oru nilattiṟ palavoḻukkaṅkaḷum ōroḻukkam palanilaṅkaḷilum vēṟupaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil nikaḻumeṉpataiyē muṉ tiṇaimayakkuṟutal eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl viḷakkiṉāraṉṟi, orukālattup pala
voḻukkan tammuḷ mayaṅkumeṉṟu tolkāppiyar yāṇṭum kūṟilar, aḵtiyalpaṉmaiyiṉ. ataṉāl iccūttiram tiṇaimayakkam kūṟuvataṉṟu, orucār uripporuḷāmpiṟa cila kūṟutalē nutaliṟṟu eṉpatu teḷiyappaṭum. aṉṟiyum itil tiṇaimayakkam kūṟutalē avar karuttāyiṉ itaṉaiyum itupōṉṟa tiṇaimayakkam nutalum piṟacūttiraṅkaḷaiyum tiṇaikkuripporuḷ kūṟuñ cūttirattiṟkumuṉ tiṇaimayakkuṟutalum uripporuḷallaṉa eṉpavaṟṟōṭu iyaipu nōkki iṇaittuk kūṟiyiruppar. avvāṟaṉṟi, tiṇaikkuripporuḷ vakai aintaiyuṅ kūṟuñ cūttirattiṟkup piṉ itu kūṟappaṭutalāl, ituvum avvaintaṉuḷaṭaṅkāta piṟa uripporuḷ vakai viḷakku vataiyē nutaliṟṟeṉpatu tēṟṟam.
īṇṭu ummai yiraṇṭum eṇṇummai.
koṇṭutalaik kaḻitalukkuc ceyyuḷ, muṉ uripporu ḷallaṉa eṉum (13ām) cūttira uraikkuṟippil kāṭṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷaik kāṇka.
pirintavaṇiraṅkalukkuc ceyyuḷ:
	numarvari ṉōrppi ṉallatu, amarvariṉ
	munnīr maṇṭila  maṉaittu māṟṟāt
	terikaṇai viṭuttalō vilaṉē:
	arimatar maḻaikkaṇ kaluḻvakai evaṉō?
ippaḻaiya urai mēṟkōṭ ceyyuḷil, tamar varavu karuti ataṉāṉērum piriviṉukkañci uṭaṉpōkkil talaimakaḷiraṅkat talaivaṉ tēṟṟuṅ kuṟippaṟika.
	ētaṅ karutā tiḷaiyōynī eṉṉōṭu
	pōtaru vāykol pukaleṉṟa - kātalarpiṉ
	vantēṉai viṭṭu maṟaintār; emarveṟuttēṟ
	kentanilai eytu miṉi!
eṉum veṇpāvil, uṭaṉpōkkiṭaiyil toṭarun talaivi tamarai avaḷmuṉ iṭaruṟap poruvatai veṟuttut talaivaṉ maṟaiya, taṟkoḷavanta tamaraimaṟuttuk kaṟpu vaṟpuṟuttun talaivi yiṉirakkam kūṟappeṟutal kāṇka.
cūttiram: 16
kalanta poḻutuṅ kāṭciyu maṉṉa.
karuttu: ituvum, aintiṇai vakuppil aṭaṅkātaṉavāy akappakutiyil uripporuḷ āvaṉa iṉṉuñ cilavaṟṟaik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
poruḷ: kalanta poḻutum = otta talaimakkaḷ tammiṭaip pālatāṇaiyiṉ mutal etirppāṭṭil nikaḻvum; (kalanta) kāṭciyum = avvāṟu talaippaṭṭār tammuḷ aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk kūṭṭik kuṟippuraikkum nāṭṭamum; aṉṉa = (mēlaiccūttirattiṟ kūṟiyatu pōla) ēṟpuḻi akavoḻukkattiṟku uripporuḷātaṟkuriyavākum.
kuṟippu: ummai yiraṇṭum muṉṉaic cūttirattil ōtiyavaṟ ṟuṭaṉ vaitteṇṇattakum  eṉpataik kuṟippatāl iṟantatu taḻīiya eccavummai. aṉṉa eṉpatu, mēṟkūṟiya koṇṭu talaik
kaḻital pirintavaṇiraṅkal eṉpaṉavaṟṟai iṅkuk kūṟiya kalanta
poḻutum kāṭciyum uripporuḷiyalpāl oppaṉa eṉpataic cuṭṭum kuṟippu muṟṟām.  kalanta eṉṉum eccattaik kāṭci yeṉpataṉōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. īṇṭup poḻutu kālam kāṭṭātu, talaippaṭṭuḻi nikaḻccikaḷukku ākupeyarām. mutaṟporuḷāṉa kālam muṉṉarē kūṟi muṭintatāl atu mīṇṭum kūṟal vēṇṭā.
iṅkuk, kalantapoḻu teṉpatu piṉkaḷaviyal iraṇṭāñ cūttiram kūṟum talaimakkaḷiṉ talaiyetirppāṭṭaiyum, kāṭci eṉpatu akkaḷaviyal aintāñ cūttiram kūṟum aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk kuṟippunāṭum nōkkattaiyum muṟaiyē kuṟikkum. kalattal eṉpatu talaippaṭutal eṉṟa poruḷatākum. orumūvēṅ kalanta kālai eṉṉuṅ kamparaṭiyum, kalantu pōrceytā rōrcilar eṉṟa kantapurāṇa aṭiyuṅ kāṇka. kalappum eṉṉātu kalanta poḻutum eṉṟatu mutaletirppaṭuṅkālattu nikaḻum kāṭci, aiyam, tuṇivu mutaliya palavum aṭaṅkutaṟporuṭṭu. iccūttirattil kāṭciyeṉpatu mutaliṟ kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyun talaippaṭṭuk kāṇpataṉṟu; atu kalantapoḻutu eṉpatil aṭaṅkum. etippaṭṭa talaimakkaḷiṭai avar aṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk kūṭṭiyuraikkum kuṟippurai nāṭum nōkkattaiyē īṇṭuk kāṭciyeṉṟār. piṉ kaḷaviyalil viḷakkappaṭumivai yiraṇṭum puṇartal mutaliya aintiṇaikaḷuḷ evaiyumākā veṉiṉum, akattuṟaippāṭṭukkaḷuḷ ivaiyum uripporuḷ kaḷāka āṭci peṟuvatāl, aintiṇaikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkāta uripporuḷ uṇmaikūṟu mivviṭattilivai iyaipu paṟṟik kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa.
	kūṟṟamō kaṇṇō piṇaiyō maṭavaral
	nōkkamim mūṉṟu muṭaittu
eṉuṅkuṟaḷil talaimakkaḷiṉ mutaṟṟalaippāṭṭil iruvarum tammuḷ aṟivuṭampaṭuttaṟkuk kuṟippaṟiya nāṭṭamiraṇṭum kūṭṭiyuraikkum peṟṟi viḷakkappaṭutal kāṇka. muṉṉalum muyaṟciyumiṉṟit tāmē tamiyarāyt talaippaṭal kalanta poḻutākum; talaippaṭṭavar, oruvar maṟṟavar kuṟippaṟiyanāṭum nōkku kalanta kāṭci (aṟiviṉāṭṭam) ām kuṟikkoṇṭu nōkkuntalaivaṉ, talaimakaḷ kuṟippunōkkā lavaḷ karuttum taṉṉatēpōṟ kātal kaṇṇiyateṉakkaṇṭu taṉakkuttāṉ tēṟṟumavaṉkūṟṟil, aiyamum, accamum teḷintu makiḻtalum oruṅku kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
mutalil avaḷkarut taṟiyāmaiyā lavaṉ añcuvatum, accamakaṟṟu mavaḷ aḷinōkkum, ataṉmēliruvarumaṟivuṭampaṭṭuḷḷam puṇarntuḻi avaṉavaḷ nalam pārāṭṭalum ākiya mūṉṟu miṉitu viḷaṅkum. kūṟṟamō eṉṟatālaccamum, kaṇṇō eṉṟatāl kaṇṇōṭumavaḷ kātal nōkkum, piṇaiyō eṉṟatāl aṟivuṭampaṭ ṭuḷaṅkalantuḻi avaṉavaḷaip pārāṭṭalum iṉitamaiyac ciṟukuṟaḷiṟ ceṟitta cevvi viyattaṟkurittu.
	aṇaṅkukol, āymayil kollō kaṉaṅkuḻai
	mātarkol mālumeṉ ṉeñcu
eṉpatilum, aṇaṅkā laiyavaccamum, mātar eṉpatālaṟivuṭam pāṭṭut teḷivum, mayil eṉpatāl pārāṭṭu moruṅkamai talaṟika.
	mayilkol, maṭavāḷkol, mānīrt tiraiyuṭ
	payilvatōr teyvaṅkol kēḷīr kuyilpayiruṅ
	kaṉṉi yiḷañāḻaṟ pūmpoḻil nōkkiya
	kaṇṇiṉ varuttumeṉ ṉeñcu	(tiṇaimoḻi. 49)
ittiṇaimoḻi veṇpāvum, neytaṉilattuk kalanta poḻutum kāṭciyumāya uripporuḷ mayakkam kūṟukiṟatu.
cūttiram: 17
mutaleṉap paṭuva tāyiru vakaittē.
karuttu: iḵtu, uripporuḷ karupporuḷkaḷ pōla vilakkukaḷ vēṇṭi viriyāmal, mutaṟporuḷ nilampoḻu tiraṇṭiṉuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉpatai valiyuṟuttal nutaliṟṟu.
poruḷ: mutaleṉappaṭuvatu = mutaṟporuḷ eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭuvatu. āyiruvakaittē = mēlē nālāvatu cūttirattil kūṟiyāṅku nilampoḻutiraṇṭi ṉiyalpeṉum iraṇṭē vakaikaḷai yāṇṭum uṭaiyatākum.
kuṟippu: īṟṟēkāram tēṟṟaṅkuṟikkum. mutal, karu, uri eṉṟa akapporuṭ pakutikaḷ mūṉṟaṉuḷ uripporuḷāvaṉa aintoḻukkamum, avaipōla akattuṟaiyiṟ ciṟanturiya talai makkaḷiṉ kātaloḻukkaṅkaḷ piṟa cilavumāka vakaipeṟu
meṉpatai innūlār mēṟ cūttiraṅkaḷil viḷakkiṉār. atu
pōlavē karupporuḷkaḷum palavakaippaṭumeṉpatai iṉi varuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟuvar. ivaiyiraṇṭum pōlātu, yāṇṭum mutaṟporuḷāvaṉa nilamum poḻutumeṉṟiraṇṭē  vakaikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkumeṉpatai iyaipu nōkki ivviṭaic cūttirattāl aiyamaṟa varaiyaṟuttu vaṟpuṟuttukiṟār. iḵtu iṟantatu kāttal, kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal, muṭintatu kāṭṭal eṉṉum uttikaḷāl amaiyu mātaliṉ, kūṟiyatu kūṟalākātu.
mutal karu uri eṉa muṉṉē muṟaippaṭuttik kāṭṭiyavar mutaṟporuḷ vakaiyiyalpukaḷai mutaliṟ kūṟi, ataiyaṭuttuk karupporuḷ kūṟāmal uripporuḷkaḷiṉ iyal kūṟiṉār, ivaiyiraṇṭum aḷavu paṭṭamaiyum iyaipu nōkki. avviyaipai valiyuṟuttum poruṭṭē īṇṭu iccūttirattāl muṭintatu kāṭṭit tantuṇipuraittār. uripporuḷaṉaittum talai makkaḷiṉ aṉpiṉaintiṇai vakaikaḷil amaintaṭaṅkum. mutaṟporuḷkaḷum nilampoḻuteṉum iraṇṭē vakaiyilaṭaṅkum. ivaiyiraṇṭum pōlātu karupporuḷvakaikaḷ eṇṇiṟantu ēṟṟapeṟṟi viriyumiyalpuṭaiyavātalāl, aḷavupaṭṭa ivaiyiraṇṭum muṟkūṟi, ivaṟṟiṉpiṉ ivaiyallāta aḷaviṟantaṉavāy varum palavum karupporuḷ vakaikaḷāku meṉpatu viḷaṅkak karupporuḷiyal iṟutiyiṟ kūṟukiṉṟār. iv aḷavu muṟaiyāṉ vaippu muṟaiyum amaikiṉṟatu. mutaṟporuḷ iraṇṭē vakaikaḷuḷ aṭaṅkuvatākaliṉ mutalil vaikkap paṭṭatu. uripporuḷkaḷ akavoḻukkaṅkaḷuḷ cilavāka aintu vakaikaḷil aṭaṅkutalāl avaṟṟai mutaṟporuḷiṉ piṉṉark kūṟi, avviru vakai yaḷavāṉum aṭaṅkāmal palapaṭaviriyum karupporuḷkaḷai avviraṇṭiṉ piṉ amaittuk kūṟiṉār.
cūttiram:18
teyva muṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟai
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokaii
avvakai piṟavuṅ karuveṉa moḻipa.
karuttu: itu mutal uripporuḷkaḷil aṭaṅkāvāy akattiṇaikku uṟuppām karupporuḷvakai uṇarttukiṟatu.
poruḷ: teyvam = vaḻipaṭu kaṭavuṭpakuti; uṇāvē = ūṇvakai; mā = vilaṅkuvakai; maram = marañceṭikoṭi vakai; puḷ = paṟavai vakai; paṟai = avvannilaṅkaḷukkuriya paṟaivakaikaḷ; ceyti = toḻilvakai; yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokaii = yāḻ vakaikaḷōṭu kūṭṭi; avvakai piṟavum = avaipōla akat
tiṇaikaḷukkuc cārpāka vakaippaṭuvaṉa maṟṟaiyaṉavum; karueṉa
moḻipa = karupporuḷkaḷkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvar akapporuṇulār,
kuṟippu: muṉṉaic cūttirak kuṟippuraiyil cuṭṭiyāṅku, mutalum uriyumallāta akapporuḷkaḷuḷ akattiṇaikkuk karuvāy amaivaṉa yāvum karupporuḷkaḷ eṉpar akap poruḷ nūlār. iccūttirattil aḷavaṟṟa karupporuḷvakai kaḷaṉaittaiyum vakuttu niṟuttal kūṭāmaiyiṉ, ciṟantaṉa cila cuṭṭi avvakai piṟavum karu eṉṟu kūṟi moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa avvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉaiyum muṭṭiṉṟu muṭiyavaittār tolkāppiyar.
cūttiram: 19
ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum
annilam poḻutoṭu vārā vāyiṉum
vanta nilattiṉ payatta vākum.
karuttu: itu karupporuṇ mayakkamum kaṭitalillai yeṉpatu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
poruḷ: ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum = enta nilac cārpil kūṟappaṭum pū, puḷ mutaliya karupporuḷkaḷum; annilam poḻutoṭu vārāvāyiṉum = avvak karupporuṭkuc ciṟanturiya nilattōṭum poḻutōṭum iyaintu vantilavāyiṉum; vanta nilattiṉ payatta ākum = kūṟappaṭṭa oḻukkanikaḻiṭat toṭu porunta ataṟkēṟṟa payaṉ taruvaṉavāy amaiyum.
kuṟippu: pūvum puḷḷum eṉpavaṟṟuḷ ummai eṇṇummai. vārāvāyiṉum eṉpatilummai yetirmaṟai cuṭṭum; karup poruḷkaḷ perumpālum tattamakkuriya nilam poḻutoṭu iyaintu varutalē ciṟanta muṟai, orōviṭattu māṟivariṉum vanta iṭattoṭu poruntap payaṉ taruvatāl kaṭiyappaṭāmal amaittuk koḷḷappaṭum eṉum poruṭṭātaliṉ.
uripporuḷkaḷaip pōlavē karupporuḷkaḷum mutaṟ poruḷkaḷāṉa nilampoḻutukaḷiṉ vakaikaḷil iyaipunōkki ēṟṟapeṟṟi orōvoṉṟiṟkē ciṟappurimaiyuṭaṉ poruntu vaṉavākum. nilampoḻutu vakaikaḷiṉ poruttam nōkki avvavaṟṟiṟkuriya karupporuḷkaḷamaiyak kūṟuvatē peruvaḻakkām. eṉiṉum, orōviṭattu nilampoḻutukaḷukku nērurimaiyallāta tiṇaikkuriya voḻukkaṅkaḷ mayaṅkakkūṟuvatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻak keṉṟu mēlē 12, 13 āvatu cūttiraṅkaḷ kūṟiṉatāl, avviṭaṅ kaḷil tammuḷiyaipiṉṟi māṟupaṭṭa mutalurip poruḷkaḷil etaṟkiyaiyak karupporuḷkaḷiṉ amaivu karutappaṭumeṉṟa  aiyam eḻuvatiyalpu. avvaiyamakaṟṟa īṇṭu iccūttiram eḻuntatu. nilam poḻutukaḷōṭu poruntak karupporuḷ kūṟuvatu peruvaḻakkiṟṟāyiṉum, payaṉnōkki akattiṇaiyiṟ ciṟanta oḻukkaṅkaḷukkiyaiya nalantaruvaṉavaṟṟai amaittuk kōṭal pāṭalcāṉṟa pulaṉeṟivaḻakkēyām eṉpatai iccūttiram valiyuṟuttukiṟatu. pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavai nāṭuṅkālai mutaliṟ karuvum karuvil uriyumē muṟai ciṟantaṉa eṉun  tamiḻmarapai muṉ mūṉṟāñ cūttirattuk kūṟiṉarātaliṉ, nilampoḻutukaḷilum ciṟanta uripporuḷoḻukkattiṟkup poruntavarum karup poruḷkaḷ mutaṟporuḷukkiyaiyāviṭattum kaṭiyappaṭā eṉṟu  itaṉāl innūlār amaivukāṭṭi vaṟpuṟuttiṉār.
māyōṉmēya eṉum muṉṉaic cūttirattil nāṉilaṅkaḷ muṟaiyē mullai, kuṟiñci, marutam, neytal eṉat tiṇaippeyarē koḷḷum eṉṟamaittatum akattuṟaikaḷil tiṇaikkuriya poruḷkaḷāṉa oḻukkaṅkaḷē ciṟappuṭaiyavātal paṟṟiyēyām. āṉāl oru nilattiṟ piṟitoḻukkam nikaḻvatāyp pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkañ ceyyumiṭattu avvannilattoṭu iyaṟ porutta millāta karupporuḷkaḷait tiṇainōkkik kūṟanēriṉ, nikaḻumoḻukkattukku akkarupporuḷkaḷ ēṟpuṭaiyaṉavāy amaiyum payaṉuṭaittātal vēṇṭumeṉṟu innūlār īṇṭu oppakkūṟiyamaittār. kāṭu, malai, ūr, kaṭal eṉpavaṟṟuḷ kūṟuvatu yātāyiṉum ataṟkiyaiyap poruttamuṭaiya karupporuḷ kaḷamaiyak kūṟutaloṉṟu, aṉṟi annilattil nikaḻvatāyk kūṟappaṭum tiṇaikkup porunta amaivatoṉṟām. karupporuḷkaḷ tamakkuriya nilam poḻutukaḷōṭu iyaiya vārā viṭaṅkaḷil, avvannilattu nikaḻum oḻukkattōṭu amaiyum payaṉuṭaiyavātal vēṇṭum. īṇṭu nilattiṉ payatta eṉpatu nikaḻum oḻukka nilattaik kuṟikkum. aḵtāvatu, annilattil nikaḻvatāyk kūṟappaṭum tiṇaiyaiccuṭṭum. (neytaṟ) kaṭaṟ karupporuḷkaḷaik kāṭṭoṭu cērakkūṟumiṭaṅkaḷil, ap poruḷkaḷ kāṭṭiṟkuriya payaṉtarumeṉpatu iccūttirak karuttaṉṟu; kāṭṭil neytaṟṟiṇai nikaḻumiṭattuk kāṭṭiṟkuriya vallāk karupporuḷkaḷvariṉ, avai āṅkē neytaṟṟiṇaik kēṟpaṉavāy amaiyak kūṟuvatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkām eṉpatē  iccūttiram nutaliya poruḷākum.
	poriyaraik kōṅkiṉ poṉmaruḷ pacuvī
	viriyiṇar vēṅkaiyoṭu vēṟupaṭa milaicci
	viravumala raṇinta vēṉiṟ kāṉyāṟṟut
	tēroṭu kuṟuka vantōṉ
	pēroṭu puṇarntaṉ ṟaṉṉaiyiva ḷuyirē	(aiṅkuṟu. 367)
itil, pālaikkuriya vēṉiṟkālattu, mullaik kāṉyāṟṟil, puṇarvuttiṇaiyil, pālaikkōṅkiṉ pacumalarōṭu kuṟiñci vēṅkaippūvum viravip puṇarntu pirintōr oḻukkoṭu poruntavanta cevviyum  vāyppum tikaḻvatu kāṇka.
cūttiram: 20
peyarum viṉaiyumeṉ ṟāyiru vakaiya
tiṇaitoṟu marīiya tiṇainilaip peyarē.
karuttu: itu, karupporuḷkaḷōṭu aṭaivuṭaiya akat tiṇaik kuriyarāṉa avvan nilamakkaḷiṉ peyarppākupāṭu kūṟukiṟatu.
poruḷ: tiṇaitoṟu marīiya tiṇainilaip peyarē = ovvō roḻukkattoṭum poruntiya mullai mutaliya nilaṅ kaḷil pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil akattiṇaikkuriyarāyk kūṟappaṭum makkaṭpeyarkaḷ; peyarum viṉaiyumeṉṟāyiruvakaiya = avvannilattāṉamaiyum peyarppeyarum, annilattu makkaḷiṉ toḻilāṉ amaiyum viṉaippeyarum, eṉṟu avviraṇṭu kūṟupāṭṭiṉaiyuṭaiyavākum.
kuṟippu: īṟṟēkāram acainilai. peyarum viṉaiyum eṉpavaṟṟuḷ ummai eṇṇummai. tiṇainilai eṉpatu ākupeyarāyt tiṇainilaiyiṉaraik kuṟikkum.
nāṉilaṅkaḷiṉum uḷḷa makkaḷ, akattiṇaikkuriyarāyc ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟappaṭuṅkāl, mullai mutaliya avvattiṇai nilaṅkaḷukkuriya iyal  iyalpuṭaiya peyar koḷḷutal oṉṟu; avvāṟaṉṟit tattam toḻiṟkiyaipuṭaiya peyar koḷḷutal oṉṟu; avvāṟaṉṟit tattam toḻiṟkiyaipuṭaiya peyar koḷḷutal oṉṟu. ivvirumuṟaikaḷē tamiḻakat tollai marapoṭu aṭaivuṭaiyavākum. tolkāppiyar kālat tamiḻulakil tamiḻariṭaip piṟappaḷavil eṉṟum uyarvu tāḻvuṭaṉ vēṟupāṭuṭaiya cātivakuppukkaḷ kiṭaiyā. ataṉāl akkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ tattam nilattukkēṟṟāṅku āyar, kuṟavar, uḻavar, maṟavar eṉṟaḻaikkap
paṭṭārkaḷ.
iṉi, oru nilattuḷḷār annilattiṟkuriya toḻil mēṟ koḷḷātu piṟitu toḻil kaiyāḷuvarāyiṉ, avaravar cey toḻil vēṟṟumaiyāl toḻil kuṟikkum ēṟpuṭai viṉaip peyarkaḷālum aḻaikkappaṭuvar. nuḷaiñar, paratavar eṉpaṉa neytaṉilañcuṭṭiya makkaṭpeyar. valaiyar, umaṇar eṉpaṉa nilañcuṭṭātu, valaivīcip piḻaikkun toḻiluṭaiyāraiyum, uppu uṇṭākki viṟkum toḻiluṭaiyāraiyum cuṭṭum viṉaip peyarkaḷ. toḻil etupurintum neytaṉilam vatipavaraip paratavar allatu nuḷaiñar eṉavaḻaṅkal oru muṟai; appōtu appeyar neytaṉilamakkaḷ eṉṉum poruṭṭākum. iṉi ennilattuṟaiyiṉum valait toḻil purintu vāḻvār eṉpataik kuṟikkuṅkāl, valaiyar eṉṉum viṉaippeyarāl attoḻiluṭaiyāraic cuṭṭuvatu orumuṟai. tamiḻakattiṟ paṇṭaik kālattil piṟappāl cāti vakuppukkaḷ illāmaiyāṉum, tamiḻ makkaḷellārum virumpiyāṅkut tattamakkēṟpuṭaiya toḻil koḷḷum urimai yuṭaiyarātalālum, nilam toḻil vakaikaḷāl vēṟupaṭiṉum uṇavu maṇaṅkaḷil vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yāṇṭum ellārum ēṟṟa peṟṟi kalantu oṉṟi vāḻntārātalāṉum, avar akavoḻukkaṅ kūṟum pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil akkālattavariṭai nilaipēṟuṟṟa nilappeyar viṉaippeyarkaḷāl tamiḻ makkaḷ aḻaikkappaṭum marapuṇmaiyait tolkāppiyar īṇṭu viḷakkip pōntār. nilampaṟṟiya makkaṭ peyar, peyarp peyar, ceyyum viṉai paṟṟiya makkaṭpeyar viṉaippeyar.
akam 110ām pāṭṭil, pōntaip pacalaiyār, tuḷaicci yait talaiviyākavum, neṭuntērūraṉait talaivaṉākavum tiṇainilaippeyar amaittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār naṟṟiṇai 45ām pāṭṭil,
	neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅku niyama mūtūrk
	kaṭuntērc celvaṉ kātaṟ ṟalaimakaṉākavum,
niṇaccuṟā vaṟutta vuṇakkal vēṇṭi, yiṉappuḷ ḷōppum pulavunāṟum paravarmakaḷ talaimakaḷākavum, ivvāṟu ivviru vēṟu nilamakkaḷ kātaṟṟalaimakkaḷāy akattiṇaikkuriyarāyamain tiruppatu iṅkuk karutattakkatu. akam 280ām pāṭṭil, paratavar makaḷait talaiviyākavum, piṟitoru nilamakaṉait talaivaṉākavum, amaittu ammūvaṉār kūṟiyatum akkālat tamiḻmarapu atuvāta lāṉ eṉpatu veḷippaṭai. iṉṉa pala paḻampāṭṭukkaḷāl paṇṭait tamiḻakattil nāṉila makkaḷuḷ maṇamum uṇavum piṟappu nilai viṉaikaḷ paṟṟi varaiyappaṭāmal kalantu kaiyāḷap
paṭṭaṉa eṉpatu teḷḷatteḷiyak koḷḷappaṭum. marapiyalil kāṇap peṟum varuṇavakaipaṟṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷ iṭaic cerukal eṉpatu piṟāṇṭu viḷakkappaṭum.
	yāyum ñāyum yārā kiyarō?
	entaiyum nuntaiyum emmuṟaik kēḷir?
	yāṉum nīyum evvaḻi yaṟitum?
	cempulap peyalnīr pōla
	aṉpuṭai neñcan tāṅkalan taṉavē.
eṉṉum kuṟuntokaip (40ām) pāṭṭāṉum piṟappu toḻil vakai karutāmal otta aṉpē maṇattiṟkup pōtiyatu eṉṉun tamiḻmarapu viḷakkamākum.
nāṉilat tamiḻmakkaḷum tammuḷ vēṟupāṭiṉṟi maṇantu koḷḷum paḻaiya vaḻakkuṇmai, cāti veṟiyalaikkum piṟkālak kōvaikaḷum palanila makkaḷiṉ kalappumaṇam kuṟittut tamiḻmarapu vaḻuvāmal akattuṟaikaḷamaittuk kūṟuvatālum valuppeṟukiṉṟatu.
	kaḻaikōṭu villiyaic ceṟṟār tiyākar kamalaiveṟpiṉ
	uḻaikōṭi cuṟṟuṅ kiriyema tūruma tūrmarutam;
	taḻaikōṭi koṇṭu camaittatem māṭai taṉittaṉiyō?
	iḻaikōṭi poṉpeṟu mēyuma tāṭai yiṟaiyavarē
			(ellappanāyiṉār tiruvārūrk kōvai. cey. 101)
ivvuṇmaiyai maṟantu tolkāppiyar nūliṉ uraikārar ippaṇṭait tamiḻilakkaṇa (nūṟ) cūttirattiṟku, avarkāla iyaipaṟṟa purāṇak kataikaḷaiyum iyalvaḻakkaṟṟa āriyak koḷkaikaḷaiyum pukuttip, poruntāp putturaikaḷ kūṟi mayaṅkavaittār. āriyaruḷ nāṉku varuṇattārukkumē tamiḻariṉ akattiṇaik kaḷaviyal  oḻukka āṭciyurimai avar tam taruma cāttiraṅkaḷilum vaḻakkilum iṉmaiyāṉum, āriya taruma nūlkaḷkūṟum uyarpiṟap purimaiyuṭaiya iṭaiyiruvaṇattār tamiḻakatteṉṟu millāmaiyāṉum, ivaruraikaḷ poruntāmai yaṟika. aṉṟiyum, tamiḻilakkaṇanūl......
 mūvar taṇpoḻil varaippil nāṟpeyarellai yakattavar vaḻaṅkum yāppiṉ vaḻiyatu eṉat tolkāppiyar tāmum, vaṭa vēṅkaṭan teṉkumariyāyiṭait tamiḻkūṟum nallulakattuvaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum āyiru mutaliṉ eḻuttuñ collum poruḷum nāṭic,centamiḻiyaṟkacivaṇiyanilattoṭumuntunūlkaṇṭumuṟaippaṭaeṇṇip pulan tokuttōṉa... tolkāppiyaṉ, eṉat tolkāppiyariṉ pāyiramum vaṟpuṟuttuvatāṉum, iṅkut tolkāppiyariṉ cūttiraṅkaḷ kuṟippaṉa ellām āriyavaruṇa aṟaṅkaḷaiyalla, tamiḻ marapum tamiḻar vaḻakkaṅkaḷumē yāmeṉpatu tēṟṟam. ivvuṇmaikaḷ iṉivaruñ cūttiraṅkaḷukkum okkum.cūttiram: 21
āyar vēṭṭuvar āṭūut tiṇaippeyar;
āvayiṉ varūuṅ kiḻavaru muḷarē.
karuttu: itu, mēṟcūttirattiṟ kūṟiya tiṇai nilaippeyar vakaikaḷum appeyaruṭaiyāriṉ akattiṇaikkurimaiyuṅ viḷakkukiṟatu.
poruḷ: āyar vēṭṭuvar āṭūuttiṇaippeyar= āyarn vaṭṭuvar eṉpaṉa āṇpāl cuṭṭum mullai nilamakkaḷiṉ tiṇai nilappeyarkaḷām; āvayiṉ varūumkiḻavarumuḷarē= annilattuakattiṇaikkurimaibkāḷpavarumuḷarām.
kuṟippu: īṟṟēkāram acainilai.
āyar eṉpatu mullainila makkaḷukkut tiṇaippeyarākum. atuvēpōl, vēṭṭuvar eṉpatum mullainila makkaṭ peyareṉṟu iḷampūraṇarum, kuṟiñci nila makkaṭ peyareṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum kūṟuvar. ivaruḷ iḷampūraṇar kūṟṟup poruṭ ciṟappuṭaiyatu. māyōṉ mēya eṉṉum muṉ 5āñ cūttirattil tiṇainilaṅkaḷil mutalil vaitteṇṇiya mullai nila makkaḷukkuriya peyarkaḷuḷ, āyareṉṉum nilappeyarum vēṭṭuvareṉṉum viṉaippeyarum annilat tāṇmakkaḷiṉ tiṇaippeyarkaḷāka iccūttirattil tolkāppiyar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉāreṉak koḷvatē peyarum viṉaiyu meṉṉum mēṟcūttirak karuttai viḷakkuvataṟku aṭaivupaṭa amaivatākum. vēṭṭuvareṉpatu mullait tiṇaimakkaḷiṉ vēṭṭu viṉai cuṭṭun tiṇaippeyarākum. ataṉāl, innūlār mēlē tiṇai nilaippeyar peyarum viṉaiyumeṉṟāyiruvakaiya eṉat tokuttuk kūṟiyatai, iccūttirattil vakuttu meynniṟuttu viḷakkiṉār.
itil tiṇaippeyareṉṟum, itaṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum varum cūttiraṅkaḷil tiṇainilaip peyareṉṟum, varuvaṉa akavoḻukkattuk kurimaikoḷvār peyaraiyē kuṟikkum. akkālat tamiḻ marapukkum, uṇmaiyulakiyal vaḻakkukku mēṟpat, tamiḻ makkaḷellārum akattiṇait tuṟaikaḷil kātaṟṟalai makkaḷātaṟ kuriyar eṉpatai innūlār iṅkup palacūttiraṅkaḷāṟ teḷikkiṉṟār. nāṭāṭcikkuriyarē akattiṇaik kiḷavit talaimakkaḷ āvataṟkuriyar pōlavum, allāta nāṉila
makkaḷum viṉaivalar aṭiyār mutalāyiṉārum aṉpiṉaintiṇait tuṟaikaḷil kiḷavittalaivar. ākār pōlavum, poruḷpaṭumāṟu iccūttiraṅkaḷukkup piṟar kūṟumurai poruntātu. talaimakkaḷ eṉpatu īṇṭu akattiṇaik kiḷavit talaimakkaḷaiyē kuṟikkum; nāṭāṭcit talaimai kuṟippatu īṇṭaikku vēṇṭappaṭā.
paratti orutti, niyamamūtūrk kaṭuntērccelvaṉ makaṉiṉ meykkātalai yikaḻntu, yām pulavu nāṟutum celaniṉṟīmō; (kaṭaliṉ) perunīr viḷaiyuḷeñ ciṟunal vāḻkkai nummoṭu puraivatō? aṉṟē eṉa maṟuppavaḷ, tamakkēṟṟa talaivartam miṉattavaruḷḷumuḷar eṉat teḷittu, emmaṉōriṟ cemmalu muṭaittē (naṟ. 45) eṉa viḷakkum tarukkurai, tamiḻar evarēṉum akattuṟaiyiṟ ṟalaimakka ḷurimaiyaṉaittu muṭaiyar eṉṉu muṇmaiyai valiyuṟuttutal kāṇka. iṉṉum itupaṟṟi mēṟ cūttira uraiyiṟ kāṭṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷuṭaṉ, piṉvarum paṇṭaic cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷaṭikaḷālum tamiḻariṭai nilam toḻil nilai piṟappu vakaikaḷāl akattiṇaikkurimai yārukkum vilakkillai yeṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. nellum uppum nērēyūrīr, koḷḷīrōveṉac cēritōṟum uppuc cumantu viṟṟuttiriyum umaṇmakaḷait talaiviyākki, ammūvaṉār kūṟiya akam 390 ām pāṭṭu ivvuṇmaiyai valiyuṟuttum.
.rhu‰ ciṟukuṭik kuṟavaṉ peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷait talaiviyākkum mālvarai eṉṉum kapilar kuṟumpāṭṭum, (kuṟun. 95)
varaiyakac ciṟutiṉaic cevvāyp pāciṉam kaṭiyum koṭicciyait talaimakaḷākkum naṟṟiṇai (134ām) pāṭṭum,
iṉṉum ituvēpōl pala paḻampāṭṭukkaḷum piṟappāṟ ciṟappetuvumillāta āyar, kuṟavar nuḷaiyar mutaliya yārum kātaṟṟalaimakka ḷātaṟkuriyar eṉpatait teḷivākkum.
